GBA renderer A/B on the board (app gbsp), 2026-10-09. Nothing merged in either fork repo.
A  = fork 07cd18f2 (master for gbsp), xtensa-68000-dynarec 6094339, GBAPROF=1
B  = fork gba-speed fc063c7c, xtensa-68000-dynarec gba-speed 8019a0a (changes 1-3 + GBAROWS counters), GBAPROF=1
Bnc = B with the GBAROWS increment compiled out (local edit of GBAPROF_ROW to ((void)0), never committed),
      to take the counters' own cost out of the comparison.
Each run: launch, `load <slot>`, 20 s read from load+2.5 s (20 GBAPROF lines), volume 0. Medians.
Scenes: Mario Kart Super Circuit slot 1 (made on the board: Peach Circuit, lap 1, A held for the whole run, the
player's slots 0 and 3 untouched), Sonic Advance (USA) slot 0 with RIGHT held, Metal Slug Advance slot 0 no input.

game        build  cpu    render/drawn  wait core1  gba_render%  IDLE1%  FPS  R(drawn)
Mario Kart  A      19.31  12.50         0.04        53           21      51   51
            B      19.26  12.87         0.05        54           19      51   51
            Bnc    19.31  12.87         0.05        54           20      51   51
Sonic Adv.  A      10.41   5.90         0.00        37.5         49      60   60
            B      10.45   6.36         0.02        40           45.5    60   60
            Bnc    10.43   6.17         0.00        39           48      60   60
Metal Slug  A       9.93   5.35         0.00        33           54.5    60   60
            B       9.95   5.66         0.02        34           53      60   60
            Bnc     9.93   5.48         0.01        33           55      60   60

Per-second render, same scene second by second (A then B):
  Mario Kart A 12.53 11.73 11.62 12.53 12.60 12.54 12.49 12.44 ...  B 12.89 12.14 12.02 12.84 12.98 12.90 12.85 12.83 ...
  The B line is above A in every one of the 20 seconds on all three games: not noise.

GBAROWS on B (rows per frame):
  Mario Kart: bottom layer 2234 | layer above flat 1324 holed 706 | sprite flat 0 holed 0
  Sonic Adv.: bottom layer 4698 | layer above flat 2893 holed 47  | sprite flat 50 holed 355
  Metal Slug: bottom layer 4640 | layer above flat 1746 holed 559 | sprite flat 77 holed 154
  (change 2's flat path is taken often - 65%/98%/76% of the rows above - and still render rises;
   change 3's sprite flat path is rare: 0%, 12%, 33%.)

Verdict: render per drawn frame RISES in B on every game: +0.37 ms (MK), +0.27 (Sonic), +0.13 (MSA) without the
counters, +0.31..+0.46 with them. cpu unchanged. FPS and R unchanged: Mario Kart is limited by the ARM side
(cpu 19.3 ms > 16.7) and core 1 still has 20% idle; Sonic and MSA already draw 60.
By the pass rule (render must fall), all three changes come out.
Per-change attribution not done: the GBAROWS print in fc063c7c needs gbaprof_rows (8019a0a), so e5cd45a and
cbb8ec6 alone do not link, and 8019a0a does not cherry-pick onto them without a conflict in video.cpp.
Correctness: webcam pictures of A and B match on all three scenes.

Installed at the end: gbsp play build from fork master 07cd18f2 (no GBAPROF), 779 504 B, gbsp.done.
Buttons in Sonic Advance on the webcam: RIGHT runs, A jumps, START pauses and resumes. SELECT has no visible
effect in Sonic, so it is not verified.
